2026-10-18T12:45:09.4830778Z   echo "CLAIM_HEAD=c75609c3a172cae8497086606abe25d7b84a6c08" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4831124Z elif [ "pull_request" = "merge_group" ]; then
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4831426Z   # A queue entry is a merge commit (merge method MERGE): its first
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4831817Z   # parent is the main tip or the entry ahead of it, its second the
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4832167Z   # pull request's head. That is the shape of a pull request's
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4832529Z   # synthetic merge, whose merge-base with its target is the same
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4832878Z   # first parent, so the drift comparison and the claim list are
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4833264Z   # read exactly as on the pull request. No second parent means the
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4833672Z   # queue's merge method changed; fail rather than guess.
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4834199Z   REF=$(git rev-parse --verify --quiet 'HEAD^1^{commit}') || REF=""
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4834636Z   PR_TIP=$(git rev-parse --verify --quiet 'HEAD^2^{commit}') || {
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4835178Z     echo "This merge_group commit has no second parent; the queue must merge with merge method MERGE."
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4835717Z     exit 1
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4835965Z   }
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4836306Z   echo "CLAIM_HEAD=${PR_TIP}" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4836644Z elif [ "" = "true" ]; then
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4837061Z   # Autofix dispatch is not a pull_request event. Compare against
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4837732Z   # main the same way a PR would, not HEAD^1 (the autofix commit's
2026-10-18T12:45:09.4838151Z   # parent), so leftover drift vs main cannot hide.
